- **login**: Named logins: plan tokens, API keys, key helpers and login pools. [Logins](../logins.md) is the guide: the kinds, how each reaches the agent, and where a login can be set. No login command prints a token value, except [`agx login token`](#agx-login-token) when you ask for it. Each tick auto-discovers the next runnable plan, hands it to /yang-toolkit:execute-plan behind an objective acceptance-criteria gate, persists the outcome, and arms the next wake-up. Propose-only by default; --unattended opts into auto execution. Hard token-budget cap, goal-drift mitigation, documented kill switch. Evaluator emits next_action (proceed | refine | restart_sprint | escalate_to_user); MAIN obeys verbatim. Append to contracts.jsonl. No max round budget; the loop runs until evaluator approves OR operator stops based on cost. Hand off to /finalize when all sprints completed. - **ltscale.fr**: Échelle de type de ligne avec ECHLTP (LTSCALE, alias ECT) d'AutoCAD en français : un facteur pour tout le dessin, pour que les pointillés se voient. Essayez-le. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ltscale.fr.md))
- **ltscale.hu**: Az AutoCAD VTLÉPTÉK (LTSCALE, rövidítése VTL) parancsa az egész rajz vonaltípuslépték-tényezőjét állítja be, hogy a szaggatott vonal minden méretben látsszon. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ltscale.hu.md))
- **ltscale.it**: Come funziona SCALATL (LTSCALE, alias CT) di AutoCAD in italiano: la scala del tipo di linea per tutto il disegno, così i tratti si vedono a ogni dimensione. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ltscale.it.md))
- **ltscale.ja**: AutoCAD の LTSCALE[線種尺度](LTS)で図面全体の線種尺度を設定し、どの大きさの図面でも破線が破線として表示されるようにします。ブラウザで無料で試せます。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ltscale.ja.md))
- **ltscale.ko**: AutoCAD 선종류 축척(LTSCALE, LTS)으로 도면 전체의 선종류 축척을 설정하여, 어떤 크기의 도면에서도 점선이 점선으로 보이게 합니다. 브라우저에서 무료로 해 볼 수 있습니다. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ltscale.ko.md))
- **ltscale.pl**: Jak zmienić skalę rodzaju linii w AutoCADzie poleceniem RLSKALA (LTSCALE, skrót RLS) dla całego rysunku, by linia kreskowa pokazywała kreski w każdej skali. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ltscale.pl.md))
- **ltscale.pt-BR**: Como o LTSCALE do AutoCAD em português, ESCALATL (alias ETL), define a escala do tipo de linha do desenho, para o tracejado aparecer. Experimente grátis. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ltscale.pt-BR.md))
- **ltscale.ru**: Как изменить масштаб типа линий в AutoCAD командой ЛМАСШТАБ (LTSCALE) для всего чертежа, чтобы штриховая линия показывала штрихи при любом размере. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ltscale.ru.md))
